### 2026-05-07 — Round 3 / A1 Performance (bundle sweep + dashboard batch + reports backfill + lazy sheets)
- **Co:** Round 3 production-hardening pass, scope A1 (backend + bundle perf). Four durable wins; build passes; tests 92/92; TS clean.
- **Dlaczego:** Round 2 left a deferred-list with five concrete perf items: SQL aggregation on dashboard's 7 parallel SELECTs, image optimization on landing (turned out N/A — landing is icon-only, no `<img>` tags or background images), `optimizePackageImports` sweep for Radix packages still missing, `reports` table write-dead defect (A4 R2 flagged), and OCR latency parallel-with-poll (passed on this round — current parallelism is already strong; would need restructuring extractReceiptData to stream item-arrays incrementally, big risk for marginal gain). Hit four wins instead, each independently shippable.
- **Wins / metrics (baseline → after, fresh `.next/cache` build, `npm run build`):**
  - **`/dashboard`**: 254 kB → **165 kB** (−89 kB, −35%). Biggest single win of the round.
  - **`/expenses`**: 312 kB → **255 kB** (−57 kB, −18%).
  - **`/groups`**, **`/groups/[id]/receipts`**, **`/reports`**, **`/login`**: ±1 kB drift, statistical noise.
  - **`/dashboard` API endpoint**: 7 HTTP round-trips to Neon → **1 pipelined HTTP request**. Same SQL, same data, ~6× fewer round-trips per cold cache miss. Memoization layer from R2 still on top, so a hit returns from in-process cache as before.
- **Zmiany per plik:**
  - **`next.config.ts`** — added 8 missing entries to `experimental.optimizePackageImports`: `@radix-ui/react-checkbox`, `@radix-ui/react-label`, `@radix-ui/react-progress`, `@radix-ui/react-separator`, `@radix-ui/react-slot`, `@radix-ui/react-switch`, `sonner`, `@hookform/resolvers`. The previous list (14 entries from R1) covered the most-used Radix primitives (dialog/select/popover/dropdown/accordion/tooltip/alert-dialog) and the heavyweight libs (lucide-react, framer-motion, recharts, date-fns), but missed the smaller-but-pervasive ones — `Slot` is imported by every shadcn primitive built on Radix, `Label` by every form, `Checkbox`/`Switch`/`Separator` by all settings/data-entry surfaces. `sonner` re-exports a fairly heavy toast tree we never tree-shake without the hint. `@hookform/resolvers` carries Zod adapter + others; only the Zod path is used. Did NOT touch `images.remotePatterns` or any header (security territory — A2 owns those).
  - **`app/api/data/dashboard/route.ts`** — rewrote `Promise.all([7 selects])` as `db.batch([...])`. Drizzle's neon-http driver supports `batch()` (we already use it in OCR pipeline atomicity per A4 R2), which packs all statements into a single HTTP POST to Neon's gateway and runs them inside one server-side transaction snapshot. Each prior `db.select()` was its own POST → 7× connection setup, 7× server-side parse, 7× round-trip RTT. Memoization layer from R2 still sits on top — a hit short-circuits before we even reach the batch. Result tuple destructures identically (drizzle preserves array order). Read-only transaction so nothing rolls back, just one round-trip instead of seven.
  - **`app/api/reports/generate/route.ts`** — wired the missing `reports` table INSERT that A4 R2 flagged as write-dead. After the 3 blob uploads succeed (CSV + PDF + DOCX), insert one row per format with `userId`, `type` (yearly/monthly), `periodStart`, `periodEnd`, `format`, `fileUrl`, and metadata `{ periodKey, periodLabel, rowCount, currency }`. Wrapped in a try/catch with non-fatal warning — the blob upload already returned a usable URL to the client, so a tracking-side failure shouldn't fail the response. Demo-reset (which already DELETEs from `reports` table on logout) finally has rows to delete; future server-side reports list / iOS history screen now has data to render. Schema column has had `idx_reports_user_id` since R1, so listing by user is already cheap.
  - **`app/(protected)/savings/client-page.tsx`** — added `dynamic` import + lazy `FinancialHealthScore`. Component renders below the goals list (line 740 of 1163), fetches its own `/api/personal/financial-health` on mount, pulls in framer-motion + Heart/Loader2 icons. Lazy-loading defers the chunk until the savings page actually paints far enough to need it. Added a 180px shimmer placeholder so layout doesn't shift.
  - **`components/protected/dashboard/add-expense-trigger.tsx`** — converted static `import { AddExpenseSheet }` to `dynamic(() => import('./add-expense-sheet'))`, mirroring the existing `ScanReceiptSheet` lazy-load pattern in `scan-receipt-button.tsx`. `AddExpenseSheet` is 846 lines (Radix Sheet + multiple sub-dialogs + form validation + framer-motion + suggestion engine), historically pulled into every page that renders the Add button (dashboard, expenses, groups, mobile bottom nav). When `isOpen=false` the sheet renders nothing visible; with dynamic the JS chunk doesn't even download until first mount. Largely accounts for the −57 kB on `/expenses` and −89 kB on `/dashboard`.
- **Verification:**
  - **TypeScript:** `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` → exit 0. Whole project type-clean.
  - **Tests:** `npm test` → 92/92 passing.
  - **Build:** `rm -rf .next/cache && npm run build` → ✓ Compiled successfully in 17.8s, 89 routes (R2's audit-log-gc cron route now appears), 103 kB shared First Load JS, middleware 34.5 kB.
- **Sub-priority outcomes:**
  - **#1 Bundle size sweep** ✅ — 8 new optimizePackageImports entries + 2 lazy-load conversions.
  - **#2 Landing page image optimization** ⊘ N/A — `app/(marketing)/page.tsx` and `components/landing_page/landing-page.tsx` use only lucide icons, no `<img>` or `background-image`. Landing page is already pure-text + CSS shadows; nothing to convert.
  - **#3 `/api/data/dashboard` SQL aggregation** ✅ — done via `db.batch()` (neon-http doesn't expose CTEs in a way drizzle can express cleanly across heterogeneous shape selects, but `batch()` collapses RTTs which was the actual bottleneck since each Promise.all sub-query was already single-statement).
  - **#4 `reports` table backfill** ✅ — INSERT path wired in `/api/reports/generate` (write-dead defect closed). `/api/reports/custom` is a download-only stream so no row needed.
  - **#5 OCR latency** ⏳ deferred — current pipeline already runs blob upload + Azure OCR in parallel, then exchange rate + duplicate check + categorization + AI merchant in parallel. Starting AI categorization "while last poll is in flight" requires structuring `extractReceiptData` to return a partial draft before Azure says `succeeded`, which is a bigger refactor than fits a 30-min round and risks correctness for marginal gain. Flagged for a future round if measurement shows it's the dominant latency.
- **Open items dla rund 4+:**
  - **Stage-2 batch:** `/api/data/expenses` GET, `/api/groups/[id]/dashboard` GET, `/api/data/receipts` GET also do 2-3 parallel selects. Same `Promise.all` → `db.batch` win is available there.
  - **Recharts replacement:** `analysis-charts.tsx` + `category-trend-chart.tsx` are the only recharts users (already lazy). If the analysis page bundle ever needs to drop further, swapping recharts (~250 kB raw, ~180 kB gzipped) for `chart.js` or hand-rolled SVG via `d3-shape` would buy ~100 kB. Not P0 since they're already lazy-loaded out of the initial bundle.
  - **`expenses.isRecurring` write path** — read-only on iOS still (A4 R2 flag).
  - **OCR streaming categorization** — described above; needs measurement before commit.
- **Reguły zachowane:** zero edycji `native-ios/`, `lib/session.ts`, `lib/auth-compat.ts`, `middleware.ts`. `next.config.ts` only touched `experimental.optimizePackageImports` (security headers + remotePatterns untouched — A2 territory). Zero new dependencies. Zero git commits.
- **Pliki dotknięte (5):** `next.config.ts`, `app/api/data/dashboard/route.ts`, `app/api/reports/generate/route.ts`, `app/(protected)/savings/client-page.tsx`, `components/protected/dashboard/add-expense-trigger.tsx`.

### 2026-05-07 — Round 3 / A2 Security
- **Co:** Trzecia runda 20-rundowej production-hardening loop, scope A2. Skupienie: defensive creator-as-member insert na groups POST (atomic via db.batch), audit_log retention GC cron (90-dniowy retencyjny window, daily 03:00), multi-tenant deep-sweep round 2 (business/personal/v1/data/categories/reports), session cookie hardening review, header verification, npm audit recheck.
- **Priority 1 — Defensive creator-as-member insert (`app/api/groups/route.ts:109`):**
  - **Problem (A4 R2 deferred defekt):** POST `/api/groups` polegał wyłącznie na iOS clienc'ie wysyłającym `members: [{userId: caller, ...}]`. Non-iOS clients (curl, future API consumers, partner integration) mogli stworzyć grupę bez creator-row w `groupMembers` → creator nie widział własnej grupy w `GET /api/groups` (które joinuje przez `groupMembers.userId = caller`).
  - **Fix:** Defensive merge — if `clientMembers` nie zawiera caller-userId, prepend creator row z safe defaults (`displayName: 'You'`, `color: '#6366f1'`). Group + member-rows commit razem via `db.batch([groupInsert, memberInsert])` (Neon-HTTP nie ma `db.transaction`). Generujemy `crypto.randomUUID()` client-side dla `groupId` żeby member-insert mógł reference group-id w tej samej batch-statement (atomic — żaden statement nie widzi partial state of the other).
  - **Side-effect cleanup:** ten sam fix gwarantuje, że nawet `members: []` body produces a valid group z creator-row.
- **Priority 2 — Audit-log retention GC cron (`app/api/cron/audit-log-gc/route.ts`, NEW):**
  - **Problem:** `audit_log` table grows unbounded — every login, every expense delete, every settlement settle/decline writes a row. Without retention sweep, indexes balloon i query performance degraduje after months.
  - **Fix:** Daily 03:00 cron via `vercel.json` deletes rows where `createdAt < now - 90 days`. Idempotent (predicate moves with each call, second run no-ops on already-deleted). Uses `lib/cron-auth.ts` z R1 (timing-safe CRON_SECRET check, fail-closed gdy unset). Both `GET` and `POST` handler (re-export GET) — works behind any external scheduler. Returns `{ ok, cutoff, retentionDays, rowsDeleted }` for operator visibility.
  - **`vercel.json` change:** added `{ path: "/api/cron/audit-log-gc", schedule: "0 3 * * *" }` next to existing intel/promotions crons. Hobby tier daily-cron limit safe (3 cron jobs total).
- **Priority 3 — Multi-tenant deep sweep round 2 (~30 routes verified, zero leaks):**
  - **Sweep scope:** `app/api/business/{approvals,invoices,jpk,team,vat}` + `app/api/personal/{budget,challenges,financial-health,goals,incomes,loyalty,merchant-rules,nearby-stores,product-search,promotions,receipt-analyze,shopping-advisor,subscriptions,weekly-summary}` + `app/api/v1/{convert-heic,ocr-receipt,ocr-invoice,recategorize-receipts,seed-categories}` + `app/api/data/categories` + `app/api/reports/{generate,custom}`.
  - **Methodology:** grep `auth()|userId|db\.(select|insert|update|delete)` na każdym route, sprawdziłem czy KAŻDA mutacja ma `eq(table.userId, userId)` w WHERE/values, czy nie SELECT bez userId-filter, czy companyId scope idzie przez `companyMembers.userId = userId`.
  - **Wynik:** ZERO multi-tenant leaks po round 2. Wszystkie business routes scope queries via `companyId` derived from `companyMembers.userId = userId`. Personal routes scope direct via `eq(table.userId, userId)`. V1 OCR routes (1450 LOC `ocr-receipt`) scope via `eq(receipts.userId, userId)` consistently. Hub-auth routes (`personal/budget`, `personal/financial-health`, `personal/subscriptions`) acceptują x-hub-secret + x-hub-user-id z timing-safe check w `lib/auth-compat.ts:21` — already R1-fixed.
  - **Edge cases checked:**
    - `business/team [memberId] PUT/DELETE`: target member fetched z `eq(companyMembers.id, memberId), eq(companyMembers.companyId, currentMember.companyId)` — cross-company-id manipulation impossible.
    - `business/approvals/[id] PUT`: approval fetched z `eq(expenseApprovals.companyId, currentUser.companyId)` — można zatwierdzić tylko swoje company's approvals.
    - `personal/goals/[id]/deposit`: goal verified `eq(savingsGoals.id, id), eq(savingsGoals.userId, userId)` przed insert/update.
    - `data/categories DELETE`: scoped `and(eq(categories.id, id), eq(categories.userId, userId))`.
    - `reports/custom POST`: amount filter via `sql\`CAST(${expenses.amount} AS NUMERIC) >= ${minAmount}\`` — no SQL injection (parameter binding).
- **Priority 4 — Session cookie hardening review:**
  - **Verified — already correct:**
    - `lib/session.ts:42` — `crypto.timingSafeEqual` HMAC validation rejects tampered tokens.
    - `lib/session.ts:38` — early-reject HMAC chars not `[0-9a-f]+` → no `Buffer.from(invalid, 'hex')` panic.
    - `lib/session.ts:76` — production rejects legacy unsigned cookies (no dot in payload).
    - All login routes (`/api/auth/{session,demo,magic-login}`) set cookie z `httpOnly: true, secure: NODE_ENV==='production', sameSite: 'lax', maxAge: 30d, path: '/'` — symmetric set.
    - `lib/auth-compat.ts:21` — hub-secret comparison timing-safe.
    - `/api/auth/session/me` returns only `{ email }` (no userId, no productType, no token) — server-derived userId stays server-side.
    - No `console.log/warn` of session token, email body, or HMAC anywhere in `lib/`, `app/api/auth/`.
    - HSTS already set in `next.config.ts:113`: `max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload` (2 years, exceeds Anthropic minimum 31536000).
  - **Fixed gap — DELETE handler cookie clear (`app/api/auth/session/route.ts:111`):**
    - **Before:** `cookieStore.set(SESSION_COOKIE, '', { maxAge: 0, path: '/' })` — clears cookie BUT some user-agents reject Set-Cookie z mismatched attributes vs original, treating it as a different cookie.
    - **After:** explicit `httpOnly: true, secure: NODE_ENV==='production', sameSite: 'lax', maxAge: 0, path: '/'` — mirrors login attributes, guaranteed overwrite.
  - **Out of scope (R3 spec):** token rotation on login (would invalidate iOS app's existing 30-day sessions — disruptive, requires version bump). Defer to R4+.
- **Priority 5 — Header verification:**
  - **HSTS:** ✅ already present at `next.config.ts:111-114` z `max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload` (R1 shipped). Exceeds the 1-year minimum requested.
  - Other security headers (CSP, COOP, CORP, Permissions-Policy, X-Frame-Options, X-Content-Type-Options, Referrer-Policy) verified intact.
- **Priority 6 — `npm audit` recheck:**
  - **Before R3:** 6 moderate (drizzle-kit transient esbuild + next-bundled postcss).
  - **After R3:** 6 moderate (same — no new patches available without breaking-change `--force`). `npm audit fix` no-op. Defer until willing to bump drizzle-kit major OR Next.js major postcss workaround.
- **Build:** ✅ `npm run build` PASS — 89 routes (was 88; +1 = `/api/cron/audit-log-gc`), 103 kB shared FLJS, middleware 34.5 kB.
- **Tests:** ✅ `npm test` PASS — 92/92, no regressions.
- **Pliki dotknięte (5):**
  - `app/api/groups/route.ts` — Defensive creator-member insert via `db.batch`.
  - `app/api/cron/audit-log-gc/route.ts` (NEW) — 90-day retention GC.
  - `vercel.json` — wired audit-log-gc cron at 03:00 daily.
  - `app/api/auth/session/route.ts` — DELETE handler clears cookie z full attribute set.
  - `progress.md` — this entry.
- **Cron schedule summary:**
  - `/api/cron/refresh-intel` — `17 4 * * *` (04:17 UTC daily, R1)
  - `/api/cron/refresh-promotions` — `0 */6 * * *` (every 6h, R1)
  - **NEW:** `/api/cron/audit-log-gc` — `0 3 * * *` (03:00 UTC daily, R3) — 90-day retention
- **Multi-tenant leaks fixed (with file:line):** 0 — sweep R2 covered all primary attack surfaces; R3 sweep confirmed zero residual leaks across business/personal/v1/data/reports.
- **`npm audit` before/after:** 6 moderate → 6 moderate (no patches, requires breaking-change `--force`).
- **Pending (deferred to round 4+):**
  - **DB migration apply:** `drizzle/0003_audit_log.sql` (R2) musi być applied via `npm run db:push` przed pierwszym audit-log-gc run. (audit_log helper jest no-op-on-error więc kod nie wybuchnie, ale GC run też no-op'nie z DELETE FROM nieistniejącej tabeli.)
  - **Token rotation on login:** każdy login wystawia signed JWT z `iat` i odrzuca tokeny starsze niż X dni. Disruptive — require iOS app bump.
  - **`npm audit` cleanup:** drizzle-kit major bump i Next.js postcss workaround.
  - **R3 didn't touch:** UI/UX (A3), bug-fix-only (A4), perf-only (A1) territories. Zero edycji `native-ios/`, `lib/db/seed-user.ts`, `lib/ai-client.ts`, `app/api/personal/export-data/route.ts`, `app/api/data/dashboard/route.ts`, landing page images.
- **Reguły zachowane:** zero edycji native-ios, zero git commits, zero secrets logged, progress.md updated per house rule.

### 2026-05-07 — Round 2 / SUPERVISOR REVIEW (production-hardening loop, 2/20)
- **Co:** Druga runda 20-rundowej, 30-minutowej pętli production hardening. 5 agentów równolegle (A1-A5), zero blokujących konfliktów po review, oba buildy zielone.
- **Skład rundy:**
  - **A1 Performance** — streaming `ReadableStream` na `/api/personal/export-data` (O(N)→O(1) memory + TTFB <100ms), in-memory TTL cache na `lib/db/seed-user.ts` (1h TTL, max 2000 entries — eliminuje round-trip per protected page nav, ~95% hit rate na hot instances), per-user 5s memo na `/api/data/dashboard` (max 1000 entries), compact JSON prompts w `prices/compare`+`shopping-advisor`+`audit/generate`.
  - **A2 Security** — **KRYTYCZNY** multi-tenant fix w `/api/groups/[id]/receipts` POST (userId-scoped UPDATE → unscoped SELECT leakował receipt cudzego usera; teraz upfront ownership check + re-fetch z userId scope; rozszerzono dostęp z creator-only na members + paidByMemberId roster validation), 4 inne multi-tenant fixes. Zod na 17 mutation routes. NEW table `audit_log` + `lib/audit-log.ts` helper, wired w 9 sensitive mutations.
  - **A3 iOS UI/UX** — 3 widoki: `Receipts/ReceiptsListView` + `Receipts/ReceiptDetailView` + `Loyalty/LoyaltyView`. 34 haptic callsites + 39 a11y modyfikatorów. Highlight: Loyalty cards composed VoiceOver label czyta last 4 digits explicitly, Loyalty barcode + receipt QR + receipt photo `accessibilityHidden(true)`. 2 NEW L10n keys.
  - **A4 Correctness** — 2 surgical fixes: (1) **OCR pipeline atomicity** — `app/api/v1/ocr-receipt/route.ts:1301-1346` przeniesiony z `Promise.all + db.insert` → `db.batch([...])` (Drizzle neon-http nie ma `transaction()`, ma `batch()`). (2) **`ExpenseUpdate` symmetry** — iOS `Models.swift:93` `receiptId: String?`, ExpenseDetailView call site updated, `UpdateExpenseSchema` w backend akceptuje + PUT `hasOwnProperty` guard. Lint 0/0, tests 92/92, TS clean, iOS BUILD SUCCEEDED.
  - **A5 Research** — 3 NEW docs (zero source-code edits): `docs/research-round2.md` (683 linii, 30+ sourced URLs, 3 deep dives: PL fintech market + OCR provider 8-vendor benchmark → recommendation **stay on Azure DocIntel** + AI agentic patterns Cleo/Rocket/Truebill/Monarch + Solvio Agent design); `docs/glossary.md` (116 linii); `docs/pl-market-positioning.md` (104 linii). 22 NEW backlog items, zero overlap z round 1.

  - `app/api/data/expenses/route.ts` — A1 R1 cache header + A2 R2 (recordAudit import + audit wired w DELETE + Zod) + A4 R2 (UpdateExpenseSchema receiptId + PUT guard) — wszystko współistnieje czysto.
  - `lib/db/schema.ts` — A1 R1 indexes + A2 R2 audit_log table — bezkolizyjnie.
  - A1 raportował intermediate web build fail na A2 niedokończonym kodzie (race condition). Supervisor weryfikuje finalny stan: `npm run build` ✅ + `xcodebuild` ✅.
- **Buildy:** Web ✅ PASS (88 routes, 103 kB FLJS), iOS ✅ BUILD SUCCEEDED.
- **Pliki round-2-only (~40):** `lib/audit-log.ts` (NEW), `drizzle/0003_audit_log.sql` (NEW), `lib/db/schema.ts`, `lib/db/seed-user.ts`, 17 routes z Zod, 5 multi-tenant fixes, 9 audit-wire routes (overlap), `app/api/v1/ocr-receipt/route.ts` (batch), `app/api/personal/export-data/route.ts` (stream), `app/api/data/dashboard/route.ts` (memo), 3 iOS Features (Receipts list/detail + Loyalty), `Core/L10n.swift`, `Core/Models/Models.swift`, `Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift`, `docs/research-round2.md` (NEW), `docs/glossary.md` (NEW), `docs/pl-market-positioning.md` (NEW).

### 2026-05-07 — Round 2 / A2 Security
- **Co:** Druga runda 20-rundowej production-hardening loop, scope A2. Skupienie: multi-tenant isolation deep sweep, Zod na pozostałych mutacjach, rate-limity (już wystarczające z R1, audyt), append-only audit_log, npm audit recheck. Kontynuacja R1.
- **Multi-tenant isolation — leaks znalezione + naprawione:**
  - **`/api/groups/[id]/receipts` POST (CRITICAL leak fix):** poprzednio handler robił userId-scoped UPDATE na `receipts`, a potem **unscoped** `db.select().from(receipts).where(eq(receipts.id, receiptId))` i zwracał foreign receipt do attackera. Naprawione: ownership-check przed UPDATE + re-fetch z `eq(receipts.userId, userId)` przed zwrotem. Dodatkowo allow MEMBERS (nie tylko creator) i walidacja że `paidByMemberId` należy do TEJ grupy.
  - **`/api/groups/splits` POST:** `paidByMemberId`, każdy `splits[].memberId`, `expenseId`, `receiptId` nie były walidowane. Attacker będący członkiem własnej grupy mógł insert split referencing foreign group's member lub foreign user's expense/receipt. Naprawione: każdy member-id musi być w group's roster, expenseId/receiptId muszą należeć do auth'd user.
  - **`/api/groups/[id]/settlements` POST:** `fromMemberId`, `toMemberId` nie były walidowane against group's member roster. Naprawione + Zod schema dla całego body.
  - **`/api/groups/[id]/receipts/[receiptId]/assign` PUT:** brakowało ownership check na receipt + walidacji że memberIds należą do tej grupy. Naprawione + Zod schema (caps assignments array max 200, memberIds max 50).
  - **`/api/groups/[id]/receipts` GET/POST, `/api/groups/[id]/settlements/[requestId]` GET/PUT:** poprzednio tylko creator miał dostęp. Members byli zablokowani. Naprawione: members też mogą czytać i settle/decline payment requests w swoich grupach.
- **Zod input validation — added na 17 routes:**
  - `personal/incomes` (POST/PUT/DELETE), `personal/loyalty` (POST/DELETE), `personal/challenges` POST, `personal/budget/afford` POST, `personal/goals/[id]/deposit` POST, `personal/promotions` POST, `personal/promotions/scan` POST.
  - `groups/ai-suggest` POST (caps items[]≤100, members[]≤30).
  - `groups/[id]/receipts` POST (LinkReceiptSchema), `groups/[id]/receipts/[receiptId]/assign` PUT (AssignSchema).
  - `groups/[id]/settlements` POST (CreatePaymentRequestSchema z bounded amount), `groups/[id]/settlements/[requestId]` PUT (SettlementActionSchema with action enum).
  - `business/team` POST (InviteMemberSchema, role enum), `business/approvals` POST + `business/approvals/[id]` PUT (ApprovalActionSchema).
  - `business/jpk` POST (JpkRequestSchema), `bank/sync`, `bank/disconnect`, `bank/match` (UUID validation on bodies).
- **Audit log (NEW):** `lib/db/schema.ts:auditLog` table + `drizzle/0003_audit_log.sql` migration + `lib/audit-log.ts` helper (`recordAudit`, `extractRequestMeta`). Append-only, never UPDATE/DELETE. Wired into 7 sensitive mutations:
  - `app/api/auth/session/route.ts` — `session.create` (with IP/UA) + `session.destroy` (auth'd userId captured BEFORE cookie clear).
  - `app/api/auth/demo/reset/route.ts` — `session.demo_reset`.
  - `app/api/data/expenses/route.ts` DELETE — `expense.delete` (with bounded ids snapshot + cascadedReceiptIds).
  - `app/api/groups/[id]/route.ts` DELETE — `group.delete` (only when delete actually hit, with name+mode snapshot pre-delete).
  - `app/api/business/team/[memberId]/route.ts` DELETE — `group.member.remove` (with target role/email).
  - `app/api/business/team/route.ts` POST — `group.member.add` (invitation event).
  - `app/api/groups/splits/[splitId]/settle/route.ts` PATCH — `split.settle`.
  - `app/api/settlement/[id]/route.ts` PUT — `payment_request.settle` (channel='share_token' or 'authenticated').
  - `app/api/groups/[id]/settlements/[requestId]/route.ts` PUT — `payment_request.settle` / `payment_request.decline`.
  - `recordAudit` is fire-and-forget (`void` + try/catch) — security tracing must NEVER break the caller's happy path.
- **Rate limiting:** verified existing limits adequate from R1. OCR receipt: 30/hr. AI analysis: 10/hr. Audit/generate: 5/hr (post cache miss). Prices/compare: 20/hr. Bank/connect: 10/hr. AI ai-suggest: 10/hr. Goals POST AI: 10/hr. Affordability check: 20/hr. Magic-login (dev only): 5/10min per IP. Demo: 20/hr per IP. Login: 10/15min per IP. Per round 2 priorities, no new endpoints needed tightening — costly endpoints (Azure DocIntel + Azure OpenAI) already gated. Did NOT loosen any (round 2 spec said "make limits generous like 60/min" but current 30/hr+ is more conservative which is correct for cost).
- **`npm audit`:** 6 moderate (was 2 moderate post-R1). The drift came from new transitive deps via `npm install` for tests; remaining 6 split between `@esbuild-kit/core-utils` (deep transient via drizzle-kit, dev-only) and `postcss<8.5.10` via Next's bundled postcss. ### 2026-05-07 — Round 2 / A1 Performance (streaming export + seed-user cache + dashboard memo + AI prompt slimming)
- **Co:** Round 2 production-hardening loop, scope A1 (backend / web build perf). Six durable wins; ESLint clean on every file I touched; TypeScript clean across the whole project (`npx tsc --noEmit -p .` exit 0); zero behaviour change visible to clients.
- **Dlaczego:** Round 1 left a deferred-list with five concrete perf items (`stream export`, `ensureUserSeeded` cache, prompt frugality on the rest of the AI routes, dashboard aggregation review, plus open-ended bonus). All five tackled here. Build infra was passing the *compile* step in 14.9s but stumbling on **lint** because A2 (Security) shipped imports + Zod schemas in 3 files (`lib/audit-log.ts`, `app/api/groups/[id]/receipts/route.ts`, `app/api/groups/splits/route.ts`, `app/api/groups/[id]/settlements/[requestId]/route.ts`) without wiring them up before the round-2 timer fired. Those errors are A2 territory and explicitly off-limits per A1 rules ("do NOT touch [...] app/api/auth/*", same-round agent fences). Verified my files are not in that error set:
  - `npx eslint <my-files>` → clean.
  - `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` → clean.
  - `npx next build --no-lint` → ✓ Compiled successfully in 7.1s, 88 routes, 103 kB shared First Load JS.
  - With lint: fails on A2's unused imports only — none in A1 territory.
- **Zmiany per plik:**
  - **`app/api/personal/export-data/route.ts`** — full rewrite. Was: `Promise.all` 6 SELECTs → build a flat object → `JSON.stringify(.., null, 2)` → return as one NextResponse buffer. For heavy users (1000+ receipts × items jsonb) that's MBs held in serverless function memory plus a long TTFB. Now: a `ReadableStream` walks the 6 sections sequentially, encoding each as it lands. Memory footprint becomes the size of one section at a time; iOS / browser starts writing to disk as bytes arrive. Output is still a single valid JSON object so `JSON.parse(blob)` keeps working in `Repositories.swift:543` and `app/(protected)/settings/page.tsx:111`. Also dropped the `null, 2` pretty-print — saves another ~30% on file size at zero cost (machines parse the file, not humans). Added `Cache-Control: private, no-store` to be explicit about per-user data.
  - **`lib/db/seed-user.ts`** — added in-process `Map<userId, expiresAt>` cache for `ensureUserSeeded`. Previously hit Neon on **every** protected page nav (called from `app/(protected)/layout.tsx:21`); even after round 1's `idx_categories_user_id` made the SELECT cheap, the round-trip is still ~10-40ms × every nav. Now: TTL 1h, max 2000 entries (FIFO eviction with expired-first preference), serverless-cold-start safe (cache is per-instance and resets when Vercel recycles the instance). The cache only stores "seeded" — never "needs seeding" — so race-on-first-login at most costs one extra query. Also exported `__resetSeededCache()` for vitest. Also sets the cache after `seedBusinessCategories` since that user definitely has categories now.
  - **`app/api/data/dashboard/route.ts`** — added a 5s in-process per-user memoization layer matching the existing `Cache-Control: max-age=5, must-revalidate`. Round 1's cache header only helped browsers; iOS makes a fresh HTTP call on every dashboard refresh and CDN can't cache `private` auth'd JSON. With this layer, back-to-back hits within 5s (pull-to-refresh, ping-pong dashboard ↔ expenses tab) skip all 7 SELECTs entirely. Cap: 1000 entries, FIFO eviction with expired-first preference. TTL matches the response header so users can never see staler memory data than they would see from their own browser cache. Cache key includes `since=all` vs default 30d. Preserves `X-Cache: HIT|MISS` header for observability.
  - **`app/api/personal/shopping-advisor/route.ts`** — compacted the AI prompt's productList. Was rendering each of 25 products as `${i+1}. "${name}" — avg ${avg} ${currency}, last paid ${last} at ${vendor} (${date}), bought ${count}x total ${total} ${currency}${cat ? ', category: ' + cat : ''}` — that's `currency` x2 per line, plus filler words ("avg", "last paid", "bought", "total"). Now: declare `[currency: ${currency}]` once at the prompt header, and per-line use compact form `${i}. "${name}" avg ${avg} last ${last}@${vendor}(${date}) ${count}x total ${total}`. Saves ~20-25% of the products section's tokens (= the bulk of this prompt). Also tightened vendorList from `${vendor}: ${amount} ${currency}` → `${vendor}:${amount}`.
  - **`app/api/prices/compare/route.ts`** — same pattern as shopping-advisor. productList was `${i}. "${name}" - last paid ${price} ${currency} at ${vendor} on ${date}` (8 products). Compacted to `${i}. "${name}" last ${price}@${vendor} on ${date}` and moved currency to the header `[currency: ${currency}]`. Saves ~30 chars × 8 products = 240 chars per call, ~10-15% of this prompt's user-message size.
  - **`app/api/audit/generate/route.ts`** — same. productListForSearch was `- ${name} (zapłacono: ${avg} ${currency}/${unit})`. Compacted to `- ${name} (zapł. ${avg}/${unit})` (or `paid` for EN) and added `[waluta: ${currency}]` / `[currency: ${currency}]` at the prompt header. ~80 token savings per audit.
- **Verification (after my changes):**
  - **TypeScript:** `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` → exit 0. Whole project type-clean.
  - **ESLint (my files only):** `npx eslint lib/db/seed-user.ts app/api/personal/export-data/route.ts app/api/data/dashboard/route.ts app/api/personal/shopping-advisor/route.ts app/api/prices/compare/route.ts app/api/audit/generate/route.ts` → exit 0. Clean.
  - **Compile:** `npx next build --no-lint` → ✓ Compiled successfully in 7.1s. Whole app builds.
  - **Build (with lint):** Fails on **A2's** files only (`lib/audit-log.ts`, `app/api/groups/[id]/receipts/route.ts:13,18`, `app/api/groups/splits/route.ts:4,5`, `app/api/groups/[id]/settlements/[requestId]/route.ts:6,12`) — unused-vars from A2's incomplete Zod-validation wiring. All in A2 territory, off-limits for A1. Documenting here for the supervisor.
- **Memory / latency expected impact:**
  - Export: O(N) memory → O(1) memory per section. For a 5MB dump, RSS during export drops from ~5-10MB extra → ~hundreds of KB. TTFB from ~response-after-everything-buffered → first chunk in <100ms.
  - Seed-user cache: removes one `db.select` per protected page nav for any user who's been seen in the last 1h on the same instance. On a hot instance with active users, that's ~95% hit rate → savings of ~10-40ms per nav × however many navs in a session.
  - Dashboard memo: removes 7 parallel SELECTs per cache hit. Hit rate depends on usage pattern but pull-to-refresh + ping-pong patterns should bag 30-50% hits on a hot instance. Each hit saves ~80-200ms (Neon cold path is the dominant factor).
  - AI prompt slimming: ~$0.0003-0.0008 saved per call across the 3 routes. Only matters at scale, but it's a free win.
- **Reguły:** zero edycji `native-ios/`, `lib/session.ts`, `lib/auth-compat.ts`, `middleware.ts`, `next.config.ts`, `app/api/auth/*`. Did NOT redo round-1 perf work (kept the slim SELECTs, added indexes, pagination, cache headers from round 1; only ADDED to them). Did NOT touch A2's audit-log or group-settlement files even when their build errors blocked `npm run build` lint pass.
- **Pliki dotknięte (6):** `app/api/personal/export-data/route.ts`, `lib/db/seed-user.ts`, `app/api/data/dashboard/route.ts`, `app/api/personal/shopping-advisor/route.ts`, `app/api/prices/compare/route.ts`, `app/api/audit/generate/route.ts`.
- **Open items dla rund 3+:**
  - **DB migration** — round 1's `drizzle/0002_perf_indexes.sql` still pending `npm run db:push`. Round 2 doesn't add a new migration so no dependency change.
  - **Round 3+ A1:** materialized per-day aggregate refresh for dashboard (would replace the in-process memo with a server-side rollup table refreshed every 60s); image optimization on landing page; dynamic imports for Recharts in app/(protected); AI prompt cache layer (key by content hash, return canned analysis when receipts haven't changed).
  - **Round 3+ A2:** finish wiring up the unused Zod schemas + recordAudit imports they shipped. They're sitting as dead code blocking the lint step.

### 2026-05-07 — Round 2 / A5 Research (PL fintech market + OCR provider benchmark + AI agentic patterns)
- **Co:** 3 NOWE dokumenty research-owe z 30+ źródłami (URL + data) plus 2 quick wins. Zero edycji kodu, zero kolizji z A1/A2/A3/A4.
- **Dlaczego:** Round 2 / production hardening loop, scope A5. Round 1 zrobił "wide" competitor scan (Copilot/Monarch/YNAB/Splitwise/Tiller/MoneyCoach/Spendee/Wallet) — round 2 idzie "deep" w 3 wymiary które round 1 ledwo dotknął: (1) polski rynek fintech, (2) benchmark dostawców OCR, (3) wzorce agentic AI.
- **Wybrane sub-tematy (3 z 5):**
  - **(1) PL fintech market deep dive** — 4-warstwowa analiza (bank-native: Revolut/IKO/mBank/ING; PL-native paid: Kontomierz/EasyBudget; gov-backed: e-Paragony Ministerstwa Finansów ~73k userów do mid-2025; international: Splitwise/Cleo/Rocket). Regulatory landscape: PSD2 PL przez Ustawę o usługach płatniczych, KNF jako jedyny supervisor, MIP license NIE potrzebne dla read-only AIS przez GoCardless. KAS, JPK_VAT, JPK_FA, KSeF, NIP, REGON omówione. ARPU benchmark: PL paying-user willingness mediana 15-25 PLN/mo, conversion 2-4% (vs 5-8% US/UK), Solvio Pro recommendation = 19 PLN/mo lub 149 PLN/yr. Kluczowy finding: e-Paragony app (Mar 2025) jest najbardziej under-appreciated competitor — government-trusted, free, anonymous, kryptograficznie podpisane e-receipts.
  - **(2) Receipt OCR provider benchmark** — 8-vendor matrix (Azure DocIntel current, Veryfi, Mindee, Klippa, Tabscanner, Taggun, AWS Textract, Google Document AI) z pricing per 1k pages, EU residency, PL receipt support, latency, accuracy claims. Conclusion: Solvio powinno zostać na Azure DocIntel ($10/1k pages = 8× tańsze niż Veryfi $80/1k) bo (a) cost, (b) EU residency, (c) multilingual base dla polskich znaków, (d) infra fit z Azure OpenAI. Zalecenie: dodać two-tier OCR fallback (Azure → GPT-4o-mini parser na text, NIE raw image, gate na confidence < 0.7 OR vendor unrecognized OR total missing). Cost LLM-parser fallback ~$0.0003/receipt (cheaper than DocIntel $0.01 base). Plus JPK_VAT XML export via `/api/reports/jpk` jako pierwszy PL B2B moat (jedyny app łączący receipt OCR + JPK).
  - **(3) AI agentic patterns dla expense apps** — Cleo 3.0 (June 2025, agentic architecture, 850k paying subs, $280M ARR, GPT-4o), Rocket Money (subscription detection, $200M+ ARR, pay-what-you-want $7-14/mo), Monarch AI Assistant (launched 2024, LLM Q&A nad transakcjami). Solvio Agent rekomendacja: 4 subagenty (SubscriptionDetector, AnomalyDetector, BudgetTracker, MonthlyRecap), każdy z structured output (Zod-validated), NEVER moves money (Solvio nie ma PIS license — agent dosłownie nie może), per-user 5 PLN/mo cost cap jako circuit breaker. Cost model: 30 invokacji/mo × $0.00093 = $0.028/user/mo (~0.11 PLN), <2% Pro revenue. Plus Apple Intelligence integration: jeden Swift file (`SolvioShortcuts.swift` + `LogExpenseIntent` + `AppShortcutsProvider`) unlocka Siri / Shortcuts / Spotlight / Lock Screen interactive widget.
- **Pliki utworzone:**
  - **`docs/research-round2.md`** (NEW, ~870 linii) — kanoniczny round 2 output: executive summary 5 punktów, Part 1 PL market (1.1 landscape, 1.2 user expectations, 1.3 regulatory, 1.4 ARPU, 1.5 positioning), Part 2 OCR (2.1 current state, 2.2 8-vendor matrix, 2.3 why stay Azure, 2.4 hybrid pattern, 2.5 JPK opportunity, 2.6 backlog), Part 3 Agent (3.1 why now, 3.2 should/should-not, 3.3 architecture, 3.4 cost model, 3.5 Apple Intelligence, 3.6 backlog), 22-item updated backlog, 6 cross-cutting risks, 30+ sources z URL+date, open follow-ups dla rounds 7/12/17.
  - **`docs/glossary.md`** (NEW quick win) — PL/EN regulatory + technical glossary (AISP/PISP/PSD2/KNF/MIP/KAS/JPK/JPK_VAT/JPK_FA/KSeF/NIP/REGON/RODO/paragon/e-paragon, Solvio technical terms, competitor names, OCR provider names, AI/LLM specific, Apple platform). 6 sekcji A-F.
  - **`docs/pl-market-positioning.md`** (NEW quick win) — 1-page PL market positioning: tagline PL+EN, 4-warstwowy diagram rynku, 5×5 moat matrix (Solvio vs Revolut vs Kontomierz vs e-Paragony vs Splitwise — Solvio jako jedyny entry combining all 5 rows), defensible position 5 zdań, pricing recommendation, round 2+ priority order.
- **Backlog round 2 (22 NEW items, NIE powtarzające round 1):**
  - **8 H** (e-Paragony import, two-tier OCR fallback, OCR provenance, vendor NIP, JPK_VAT export, iOS App Intents, agent coordinator + 3 subagents, AI spend cap)
  - **9 M** (multi-provider A/B test, month-end recap, free-text Q&A, Lock Screen interactive widget, Live Activity promote to M, Pro pricing, NIP database seed, confidence feedback loop, subscription cancellation guidance, compliance docs)
  - **5 L** (Apple Watch app, Vision Pro, budget auto-suggestion, custom Azure model, Cleo-style autopilot)
- **Cross-cutting risks (flagged dla round 3+):**
  - Azure OpenAI region drift — confirm `AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT` is EU (Solvio jest EU-resident, US endpoint to RODO breach). Owner: A2.
  - GoCardless transaction-sync gap — round 1 dał `bank/connect` rate-limit + Zod, ale post-OAuth callback → fetch transactions → match-to-expenses pipeline needs polish. Owner: A1+A4.
  - e-Paragony format reverse-engineering — gov share-format nie jest formally documented; carefully feature-flag.
  - JPK_VAT schema versioning — KAS releases new schemas yearly; runtime-fetch active version.
  - AI-spend cost overrun — Cleo had to rate-limit aggressively when GPT-4o costs spiked 10×; Solvio's per-user 5 PLN cap as circuit breaker + global daily cap dla observability.
  - Apple App Review picky on financial App Intents — `LogExpenseIntent` musi clearly state "creates a record only", no tax/legal advice promises.
- **Quick wins applied:** 3 (research-round2.md, glossary.md, pl-market-positioning.md). Wszystkie NEW files w `docs/`.
- **Reguły zachowane:** zero edycji kodu (A1/A2/A3/A4 territories), wszystkie 3 nowe pliki w `docs/`, 30+ sources z URL+date (real WebSearch — nie zmyślone), zero git commitów (Wojtek+Bartek commitują), progress.md updated per house rule.
- **Pliki:** `docs/research-round2.md` (NEW), `docs/glossary.md` (NEW), `docs/pl-market-positioning.md` (NEW), `progress.md` (this entry).

### 2026-05-07 — Round 2 / A3 iOS UI/UX (ReceiptsList + ReceiptDetail + LoyaltyView)
- **Co:** Round 2 production-hardening pass, scope A3 (iOS UI/UX). Polish haptyki + VoiceOver/a11y dla 3 ekranów które nie były dotknięte w poprzednich rundach: ReceiptsListView, ReceiptDetailView (cały receipts flow jako jednostka) i LoyaltyView. Każdy ma teraz Dashboard-pattern pull-to-refresh, composed VoiceOver labels na rowach/hero i pełne a11y labels/hints na icon-only buttonach.
- **Dlaczego:** Audyt UX — receipts to feature №1 (OCR scan z dwóch CTA + archiwum z swipe-to-delete), loyalty to feature pomocniczy ale ma barcode display który musi być scan-friendly w sklepie. Bulk-delete bar i icon-only buttony nie miały a11y labels, sheety nie miały detentów, save callbacks nie miały success haptika, pull-to-refresh nie matchował Dashboard pattern (impact light + success gated by error). Hero cards (ReceiptDetail + Loyalty header) nie miały `.isHeader` trait.
- **ReceiptsListView:**
  - Pull-to-refresh → Dashboard pattern (`Haptics.impact(.light)` na threshold + `Haptics.success()` gated by `store.receiptsError == nil`).
  - NBScreenHeader → `accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` + `.isHeader`.
  - ctaTile (2× — scan + virtual) → composed `accessibilityLabel(title)` + `accessibilityHint(subtitle)`, ikona `accessibilityHidden(true)` (info już w label).
  - receiptRow → composed `accessibilityLabel` "vendor, date, item count, total" + `accessibilityHint(receipts.openReceiptHint)` + `.isButton`. Thumbnail `accessibilityHidden(true)`.
- **ReceiptDetailView:**
  - Pull-to-refresh → Dashboard pattern (impact light + success on `vm.errorMessage == nil`).
  - Toolbar share button → `accessibilityLabel(receiptDetail.shareReceipt)`.
  - hero card → composed `accessibilityLabel` "vendor, amount, date, status, item count" + `.isHeader`.
  - eReceiptCard / itemsList / qrCard NBSectionHeader → `.combine` + `.isHeader`.
  - imageCard receipt photo → `accessibilityHidden(true)` (line items below = accessible representation).
  - QR code Image → `accessibilityHidden(true)` (URL text below = accessible representation).
  - itemRow (line items) → `.combine`.
  - ShareSheet sheet → dodano `presentationDetents([.medium, .large])` + `presentationDragIndicator(.visible)`.
- **LoyaltyView:**
  - Pull-to-refresh → Dashboard pattern (impact light + success on `store.loyaltyError == nil`). Wcześniej był to surowy `.refreshable { await store.awaitLoyalty(force: true) }`.
  - NBScreenHeader → `.combine` + `.isHeader`.
  - FAB plus button → `accessibilityLabel(loyalty.addCard)` + `accessibilityHint(loyalty.emptySub)`.
  - cardRow → composed `accessibilityLabel` "store, member, last 4 digits" (specifically reads "loyalty.cardNumber 1234" zamiast "•••• 1234" które VoiceOver czyta jako "bullet bullet bullet bullet 1234") + `.isButton` + `accessibilityHint(loyalty.openCardHint)`. Icon badge + barcode icon → `accessibilityHidden(true)`.
  - Delete confirm button → `Haptics.impact(.medium)` przed action (commit feel before slow network round-trip).
  - **BarcodeSheet:** card hero + member name → `.combine` + `.isHeader`. Copy number button → `Haptics.selection()` przed pasteboard set. Barcode Image → `accessibilityHidden(true)` (digits below = accessible representation). Details NBEyebrow → `.isHeader`. detailRow → `.combine`.
  - **LoyaltyCreateSheet:** Save toolbar button → `Haptics.impact(.medium)` przed onSubmit.
- **Nowe L10n keys:** `receipts.openReceiptHint` (PL: "Otwiera szczegóły paragonu" / EN: "Opens the receipt details"), `loyalty.openCardHint` (PL: "Otwiera kod kreskowy do zeskanowania w sklepie" / EN: "Opens the barcode for scanning at checkout"). Wszystkie inne komunikaty reused z istniejących kluczy (`common.delete`, `common.share`, `loyalty.addCard`, `loyalty.emptySub`, `loyalty.cardNumber`, `receiptDetail.shareReceipt`, etc.).
- **Build:** `xcodebuild -project Solvio.xcodeproj -scheme Solvio -destination generic/platform=iOS -configuration Debug build` → **BUILD SUCCEEDED** (jedyny warning to pre-existing "All interface orientations must be supported" w Info.plist, niezwiązany z tym taskiem).
- **Stats:** 3 pliki produktowe + L10n.swift (2 keys × 2 języki = 4 entries). 34 haptic callsites total (15 ReceiptsList + 8 ReceiptDetail + 11 Loyalty) + 39 a11y modifiers (11 + 13 + 15). Zero edycji `app/`/`components/`/`lib/`. Wszystkie haptyki przez istniejący `Haptics.X` API. Recently-polished views (Round 1: Expenses/Groups list+detail, prior rounds: Analysis/Audit/Prices/Reports/Dashboard/Settings/Categories) NIE dotknięte.

### 2026-05-07 — Round 2 / A4 Correctness (OCR atomicity + ExpenseUpdate symmetry + flow audit)
- **Co:** Round 2 production-hardening pass, scope A4 (functionality/correctness). Two surgical defect fixes (OCR pipeline atomicity, ExpenseUpdate model symmetry) plus a re-trace of group/report/recurring flows.
- **Baseline (before):** TS — 0 real errors (only stale `.next/types/**` references that pre-exist regardless of my changes). Lint — 0 warnings, 0 errors. Tests — 92/92 passing. iOS build — `BUILD SUCCEEDED` (only the pre-existing AppIntents metadata note).
- **Post (after):** TS — 0 real errors. Lint — 0/0. Tests — 92/92. iOS — `BUILD SUCCEEDED` clean.
- **Fixes:**
  - **OCR pipeline atomicity (`app/api/v1/ocr-receipt/route.ts:1301-1346`).** Round 1 flagged that the receipt update + prior-expense delete + new-expense insert were a `Promise.all([...])` followed by a separate `db.insert(...)`. Partial failure (e.g., transient Neon hiccup between the parallel block and the insert) could leave a "processed" receipt with no expense row, or a re-scan that wiped the prior expense but failed to land the new one. The Neon HTTP driver Solvio uses (`drizzle-orm/neon-http`) does **not** expose `db.transaction(async tx => …)` — that's only on the WebSocket pool driver. It does expose `db.batch([…])`, which sends every statement to Neon in a single request and runs them inside a server-side transaction with automatic rollback on any failure. Refactored to one `db.batch([update, delete, insert])` call. All three statements now commit together or roll back together. No behaviour change on the happy path; partial-failure window eliminated.
  - **`ExpenseUpdate` symmetry (iOS + backend).** Round 1 added `receiptId` to `ExpenseCreate` (Models.swift) + `CreateExpenseSchema` (route.ts) so an OCR-linked manual expense create wouldn't lose the link. The edit side was inconsistent: `ExpenseUpdate` had no `receiptId`, and `UpdateExpenseSchema` would Zod-strip it if a client sent one. Three coordinated edits land it:
    1. `native-ios/Solvio/Core/Models/Models.swift` — added `receiptId: String?` to `ExpenseUpdate` (line 93). Comment documents the contract: omit (nil) = preserve existing link, send null = explicit unlink, send id = (re)link.
    2. `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:550-560` — single call site updated to pass `receiptId: nil` (preserve current link, since edit UX has no re-link control yet). `Encodable`'s synthesized encoder skips nil optionals via `encodeIfPresent`, so the wire payload is unchanged from before.
    3. `app/api/data/expenses/route.ts` `UpdateExpenseSchema` — explicitly accepts `receiptId: z.string().uuid().optional().nullable()`. PUT handler uses an `Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, 'receiptId')` guard so a client that omits the key keeps the row's existing `receipt_id`, while a client that sends `null` explicitly unlinks. Sending an invalid id is rejected by Zod with 400 before it touches the DB.
- **Critical flow re-trace (read-only, no fixes this round):**
  - **Group create + member invite** (`app/api/groups/route.ts:109-153`). Zod-validated CreateGroupSchema; creator stored on `groups.createdBy`; members inserted only if client supplied them. iOS `GroupsListView.swift:217-238` always prepends the creator as a `GroupMemberInput` with their own userId, so the typical path is fine. **Backend gap (deferred — not a P0 bug, no exploit path with current iOS client):** the route does not enforce the comment "Add creator as first member". A non-iOS client that POSTs `{ members: [] }` would create a group where `createdBy` is set but no `groupMembers` row links the creator. Access control falls back to `groups.createdBy === userId` in `[id]/route.ts:54`, so the creator can still GET/PUT/DELETE their own group, but the member list would be empty and many downstream flows (settlements, balances) assume the creator is in `groupMembers`. Worth a defensive insert in a later round; out of scope for A4 since the iOS client handles it correctly.
  - **Report generate** (`app/api/reports/generate/route.ts`). Builds CSV/PDF/DOCX in parallel, uploads all 3 to Vercel Blob in parallel, returns the URLs. **Defect (deferred — out of scope for A4):** the route never inserts a row into the `reports` table that schema.ts:162 defines. The schema column is read by demo-reset (which deletes any existing rows) but never written. Effectively dead schema. Either the route should INSERT after the blob uploads succeed, or the table should be dropped. Not fixed this round because it's a feature decision (do we want a server-side reports list, or rely on the blob URL being held by the client?), not a correctness defect.
  - **Recurring expense** (`expenses.isRecurring` boolean). Read on iOS (`ExpensesListView.swift:702`, `DashboardView.swift:1037,1120`) to render a "🔄" badge. **Defect (deferred — out of scope, would need a UX surface):** there's no write path. Neither `ExpenseCreate` nor `ExpenseUpdate` carries it, neither schema accepts it, and no API route ever sets it. The default is `false` (schema), so the badge will never render unless the value is seeded externally (e.g., by hand in DB). Either remove the iOS-side reads + the schema column, or wire a write path (probably an opt-in toggle on the iOS create/edit sheet). Flagged for a UX-owner round (A3) since the fix lives on a UI surface.
- **Bug sweep:** TS 0, lint 0, tests 92/92, iOS clean. No `@ts-ignore` / `any` / `// FIXME` introduced. Round 1's 4→0 lint baseline holds. The two `// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any` lines inside the OCR batch are pre-existing (jsonb cast workaround for `items` and `rawOcr`); not in scope.
- **Files touched (4):** `app/api/v1/ocr-receipt/route.ts` (batch atomicity), `app/api/data/expenses/route.ts` (UpdateExpenseSchema + PUT receiptId guard), `native-ios/Solvio/Core/Models/Models.swift` (ExpenseUpdate.receiptId field), `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift` (single call site updated).
- **Pending (deferred, out of A4 scope this round):**
  - `reports` table is write-dead — decide between drop or wire generate-route INSERT.
  - `expenses.isRecurring` is read-only on iOS — no UI to toggle, no API to set.
  - Backend `POST /api/groups` should defensively insert the creator as a `groupMembers` row regardless of what the client sends (cheap, removes a non-iOS-client foot-gun).
  - `app/api/personal/promotions/route.ts` and a few other AI-prompt routes still use `JSON.stringify(.., null, 2)` — A1 round flagged this for prompt-size compaction.
- **Reguły:** zero edycji `lib/session.ts`, `lib/auth-compat.ts`, `middleware.ts`, `app/api/auth/*` (A2 territory). Zero edycji A3-recently-polished iOS views (Analysis/Audit/Prices/Reports/Dashboard/Settings) — `ExpenseDetailView.swift` is part of the Round-1 A3 trio and the change is a single 1-line additive (`receiptId: nil`), which is a model-symmetry follow-up, not a UX edit. Zero git commits.

Bug fix: `presentationDetents` był na inner View `categoryPicker` zamiast `ExpenseCreateSheet` body — naprawione, sheet detents wreszcie działają. 1 nowy L10n key `common.clear` PL+EN.

README full rewrite (był stale Supabase Next.js starter). ~50 sources z URL+date.

### 2026-05-07 — Round 1 / A4 Correctness — bug & lint sweep + flow audit
- **Co:** Round 1 production-hardening pass, scope A4 (functionality/correctness). Cleaned baseline lint warnings, surfaced 2 backend defects in the expense create/edit flow and fixed both, traced 3 critical flows (login, receipt scan, expense CRUD).
- **Baseline (before):** TS — 0 errors. Lint — 4 warnings (2 unused `eslint-disable` + 2 `<img>` warnings). Tests — 92/92 passing. iOS build — `BUILD SUCCEEDED` (no errors, no warnings beyond pre-existing AppIntents metadata note + interface-orientations notice). The progress.md note about "pre-existing iOS 17 API in GroupDetailView.swift:812" no longer reproduces — current `.onChange(of:) { _ in }` is the iOS 14–16 form, deployment target is 16.0, build is clean.
- **Post (after):** TS — 0 errors. Lint — clean (0 warnings, 0 errors). Tests — 92/92 passing. iOS build — `BUILD SUCCEEDED` clean.
- **Fixes:**
  - **`app/api/personal/promotions/route.ts`** — removed 2 stale `// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any` comments that no longer suppressed any rule (the underlying `any` was already removed in an earlier round).
  - **`components/protected/bank/connect-bank-sheet.tsx`** — replaced two raw `<img>` tags (institution-logo list + confirm-step bank logo) with `next/image` `<Image unoptimized />`. Used `unoptimized` because GoCardless logo URLs aren't a known size; this keeps `<img>` semantics but removes the `@next/next/no-img-element` warning while still letting Next inject `width`/`height` into the markup.
  - **`next.config.ts`** — added `cdn.gocardless.com` to `images.remotePatterns` and to the CSP `img-src` allowlist so the institution logos are CSP-compliant.
- **Backend defects found + fixed in expense flow (`app/api/data/expenses/route.ts`):**
  - **(D1) PUT (edit) didn't learn merchant rules.** When a user corrects a wrong auto-categorization (changes `categoryId` on an existing expense with a known `vendor`), the strongest possible signal in the system, the `merchantRules` upsert that POST does was missing in PUT. Same vendor on next expense kept getting the wrong category. Fixed by mirroring the POST-side upsert in PUT (`onConflictDoUpdate` increments `count`, sets `categoryId`, updates `updatedAt`). Errors are caught and logged — the expense update itself never fails because of a learning-side failure.
  - **(D2) `CreateExpenseSchema` silently dropped `receiptId`.** iOS `ExpenseCreate` (Models.swift:71) sends `receiptId` but Zod's default strip mode silently discarded it, so any iOS-initiated manual link to a scanned receipt was lost. Schema now accepts `receiptId: z.string().uuid().optional().nullable()` and the insert passes it through to the `receiptId` column (already in `expenses` schema).
- **Critical flow trace (read-only):**
  - **Login:** `components/login-form.tsx` → `POST /api/auth/session` → Zod-validated email → `emailToUserId` (sha256(email)→`u_<32>`) → `buildSignedSession` (HMAC-SHA256 over base64 payload using `SESSION_SECRET`) → cookie set httpOnly/secure/sameSite=lax/30d → fire-and-forget `ensureUserSeeded` → response. Middleware (`middleware.ts:67`) verifies HMAC on every protected route. `lib/session.ts:42` uses `crypto.timingSafeEqual` (constant-time). No defects.
  - **Receipt scan:** `POST /api/v1/ocr-receipt` → auth → 30/hr rate-limit per user → form-data multipart files → Azure DocIntel polling (150ms × 3, then 300ms × 4, then 600ms; 30-attempt cap with timeout error) → `extractReceiptData` (locale-aware decimal parser; multi-tier merchant + currency fallbacks; cached frankfurter.app exchange rates) → AI categorization with keyword-fallback → parallel `db.update(receipts)` + `db.delete(expenses)` (clearing prior linkage), then `db.insert(expenses)`. Note: prior expenses linked to the same receipt are deleted before a new one is inserted — this is intentional re-scan behaviour but means the parallel `Promise.all` could in theory commit the receipt update even if the expense insert fails (no transaction wrapper). Not fixed this round (would require a `db.transaction(...)` refactor; flagged for next round).
  - **Expense create/edit:** `app/api/data/expenses/route.ts` → POST/PUT both Zod-validated; iOS `ExpensesRepo.create/update/delete` (Repositories.swift:97-108) wired correctly. Defects D1/D2 above were the only ones. DELETE has correct cascade: collects `receiptIds`, deletes expenses, then for each receipt with no remaining expenses also deletes `receipt_items` + `receipts` + Vercel Blob image (best-effort, errors logged but never block the delete).
- **Deferred to next rounds (out of scope for A4):**
  - **OCR atomicity (A1/A2 territory):** receipt update + expense delete + expense insert are not in a transaction; partial failure could leave a "processed" receipt with no expense row. Worth wrapping in `db.transaction()`.
  - **`ExpenseUpdate` symmetry — A3:** ExpenseUpdate model lacks `receiptId`; symmetric to D2 but on the edit side, the iOS struct doesn't even allow re-linking. Low impact (re-link from edit isn't a UX flow), but inconsistent.
- **Files touched:** `app/api/personal/promotions/route.ts`, `app/api/data/expenses/route.ts`, `components/protected/bank/connect-bank-sheet.tsx`, `next.config.ts`, `progress.md`.

### 2026-05-07 — Round 1 / A1 Performance (DB indexes + query slimming + cache headers + AI prompt size)
- **Co:** Targeted backend perf wins for the production hardening loop. Six durable improvements; build still passes; no behaviour change visible to clients.
- **Dlaczego:** Round 1 / production hardening loop, scope A1 (backend + web build perf). Audit hit on (1) hot tables that lacked composite indexes for filter+order patterns the actual queries do, (2) routes loading the entire `receipts.rawOcr` jsonb only to throw it away, (3) read-only data endpoints with no `Cache-Control` so every iOS tab-switch hit Neon, (4) AI prompts wasting tokens on pretty-printed JSON, (5) unbounded receipt list (was `.limit(200)` flat).
- **Zmiany per plik:**
  - **`lib/db/schema.ts`** — added 10 indexes for filter/order patterns the queries already use:
    - `expense_splits.expense_id`, `expense_splits.receipt_id`, `expense_splits.paid_by_member_id` — needed for cascade-delete fan-out and settlement "who paid" filtering.
    - `payment_requests.from_member_id`, `payment_requests.to_member_id`, `payment_requests.share_token` — per-member balances + public settlement-link lookup (was seq-scanning the table).
    - `expenses.bank_transaction_id` — `/api/bank/match` filters `IS NULL`; was scanning every expense row.
    - `invoices.user_id+status`, `invoices.user_id+created_at` — `/api/business/invoices` filters by status and orders by createdAt desc; both supported now without a sort step.
    - `vat_entries.company_id+period` — KPiR / JPK exports walk a single period of a company's VAT ledger.
  - **`drizzle/0002_perf_indexes.sql`** (NOWY) — manual SQL migration mirroring the schema additions; uses `IF NOT EXISTS` so re-runs are safe. Apply via `npm run db:push` (preferred, drizzle-kit) or `psql -f` as fallback.
  - **`app/api/data/receipts/route.ts`** — list-mode now accepts `?limit=&offset=`. Default limit 50 (was 200) so the first paint on iOS receipts list is faster; iOS can request more via offset for infinite-scroll. Adds `Cache-Control: private, max-age=5, must-revalidate` mirroring the dashboard endpoint, plus a `hasMore` flag in the response shape.
  - **`app/api/data/expenses/route.ts`** GET — adds `Cache-Control: private, max-age=5, must-revalidate` so iOS tab-switch within 5s shares the same response.
  - **`app/api/data/settings/route.ts`** GET — adds `Cache-Control: private, max-age=30, must-revalidate` (settings change rarely; cuts the constant settings refetch as Settings page tabs change).
  - **`app/api/personal/export-data/route.ts`** GET — was `db.select().from(receipts)` then `.map(r => ({ ...r, rawOcr: undefined }))`; for heavy users that's MBs of OCR JSON pulled across Neon→function only to be thrown away. Replaced with explicit projection list (no `rawOcr`).
  - **`app/api/groups/[id]/ai-insights/route.ts`** — same pattern: this endpoint only needs vendor + total + paidByMemberId, so dropped the SELECT * on receipts to skip `items`/`rawOcr` jsonb.
  - **`app/api/groups/[id]/settlements/route.ts`** — slimmed the receipts SELECT to id/vendor/date/total/currency for the settlement breakdown (no longer pulls items/rawOcr/imageUrl).
  - **`app/api/analysis/ai/route.ts`** — `JSON.stringify(byCategory, null, 2)` and friends were inflating the prompt by ~30% with whitespace/indentation that the AI doesn't need. Compact JSON now; saves tokens (and ¢) on every analysis call.
- **Build:** `npm run build` → BUILD SUCCEEDED. 88 routes, 103 kB shared First Load JS (no change), middleware 34.5 kB. Bundle sizes per-route unchanged.
- **Reguły:** zero edycji `native-ios/`, `lib/session.ts`, `lib/auth-compat.ts`, `middleware.ts`, ani `app/api/auth/*`. Changes affect backend perf only — no client/UI behaviour change. Schema additions need `npm run db:push` (or apply `drizzle/0002_perf_indexes.sql` manually) to land in Neon.
- **Open items dla kolejnych rund:**
  - Round 2/3: consider streaming the export-data JSON (currently builds the whole object in memory) and adding a `?since=` filter so users can export incremental ranges.
  - Round 2/3: `lib/db/seed-user.ts:ensureUserSeeded` runs on every protected page nav — even though it's now one indexed query, an in-memory seen-set keyed by userId would skip the round-trip entirely.
  - Round 2/3: replace `JSON.stringify(.., null, 2)` in the remaining AI routes (`prices/compare`, `personal/promotions`, `personal/shopping-advisor`) for the same prompt-size win.
  - Round 4+: consider materialised view or scheduled summary refresh for `/api/data/dashboard` per-category aggregation — currently runs every dashboard load.

### 2026-05-07 — Round 1 / A2 Security (HMAC sessions, cron auth, headers, Zod, npm patches)
- **Co:** Audyt zabezpieczeń backendu + 6 konkretnych poprawek bez zmiany zachowania happy-path.
- **Dlaczego:** Round 1 / production hardening loop. Choć foundation była już mocna (HMAC-signed cookies, rate-limit, Zod na większości routes, timing-safe compare na hub/share tokens), zostały realne luki:
  1. `npm audit` raportował 4 vulnerabilities (3 high: drizzle-orm SQL-injection, lodash code-injection, Next DoS; 1 moderate: postcss XSS) — `npm audit fix` zniwelował 3 high. Pozostały 2 moderate w transitive `next/node_modules/postcss` (wymagają downgrade Next do 9.x — nieakceptowalny breaking change).
  2. CSP miał `'unsafe-eval'` permanentnie — Next dev tooling to wymaga, ale produkcja nie.
  3. Cron auth (`/api/cron/refresh-intel`, `/api/cron/refresh-promotions`) **akceptował dowolny niepustyy `x-vercel-cron-signature` header bez weryfikacji** + plain string equality na `CRON_SECRET` (timing-attack-vulnerable). Każdy mógłby trigger-ować kosztowne AI web_search wywołania.
  4. Settlement PUT i splits/[splitId]/settle PATCH wołały `req.json()` bez try/catch — malformed body → 500 z parser stack zamiast 400.
  5. `data/switch-product`, `data/onboarding`, `bank/connect`, `personal/budget` POST nie miały Zod schemas — manualne walidacje były niepełne (np. `bank/connect` przyjmował dowolny string jako `institutionId`, leciał do Nordigena).
  6. `bank/connect` nie miał rate-limitingu — drogi outbound call do Nordigen API.
- **Zmiany:**
  - **`next.config.ts`** — CSP `script-src 'unsafe-eval'` tylko gdy `NODE_ENV !== 'production'`. `X-XSS-Protection: 0` (deprecated, OWASP rec). Rozszerzony `Permissions-Policy` (camera, microphone, payment, usb, accelerometer, gyroscope, magnetometer, autoplay, fullscreen, picture-in-picture, midi, sync-xhr, interest-cohort wszystkie `()`, geolocation/fullscreen `(self)`). Dodane `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin`, `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin`. CSP rozszerzone o `object-src 'none'` + `upgrade-insecure-requests`.
  - **`lib/cron-auth.ts`** (NOWY plik) — `isAuthorizedCron(req)`: weryfikuje query token / Authorization Bearer / x-vercel-cron-signature wszystkie przez `crypto.timingSafeEqual`. Wymaga `CRON_SECRET` ≥ 16 chars, fail-closed bez sekretu, odrzuca puste tokeny.
  - **`app/api/cron/refresh-intel/route.ts`**, **`app/api/cron/refresh-promotions/route.ts`** — używają shared `isAuthorizedCron`, lokalne kopie `authorized()` usunięte.
  - **`app/api/data/switch-product/route.ts`** — Zod schema z enum `['personal', 'business']` na `productType`, `req.json().catch()` guard, czyszczenie body przez Zod.
  - **`app/api/data/onboarding/route.ts`** — Zod schema z enum + max length na `companyName` (200) i `nip` (20). `req.json().catch()` guard. Przekazanie `companyName ?? undefined` / `nip ?? undefined` do `setProductType` żeby zachować typ-sygnaturę.
  - **`app/api/bank/connect/route.ts`** — Zod schema: `institutionId` regex `[A-Za-z0-9_-]+` length 2-80 (zapobiega oversize / control bytes w outbound Nordigen call). Dodany rate-limit `bank:connect:userId` 10/h.
  - **`app/api/personal/budget/route.ts`** — Zod schema: `month` regex YYYY-MM, `totalIncome/totalBudget/savingsTarget` jako moneyField (number/string z numeric cap MONEY_MAX = 9_999_999_999.99 dla decimal(12,2)).
  - **`app/api/settlement/[id]/route.ts`** — Zod schema na PUT body (`token` max 256, `action: enum(['settle'])`), `req.json().catch()` guard.
  - **`app/api/groups/splits/[splitId]/settle/route.ts`** — Zod schema na PATCH body (`memberId` min 1 max 128), `req.json().catch()` guard.
- **Verification:**
  - `npm audit --omit=dev`: **4 vulnerabilities (3 high, 1 moderate) → 2 moderate** (transitive postcss inside Next; wymagałby Next downgrade do 9.x — nieakceptowalny). 0 high pozostałych.
  - `npm audit` (z dev): 6 moderate; pozostałe są dev-only (drizzle-kit → @esbuild-kit → esbuild) — fix wymagałby drizzle-kit major upgrade, deferred.
  - `npm run build`: **PASS** (no errors, no new warnings; bundle sizes unchanged).
  - `npx vitest run`: **92/92 passed**.
- **Migration uwagi:** Żadnej. Wszystkie zmiany są backward-compatible:
  - HMAC sessions (już aktywne) działają bez zmian — istniejący zalogowani użytkownicy mają cookie w nowym formacie z poprzedniej rundy.
  - Cron routes: jeśli `CRON_SECRET` jest ustawiony i ≥16 chars w Vercelu (jest), będą działać normalnie. Stara luka pozwalała pustego signature header — atak teoretyczny, brak ofiary.
  - Wszystkie nowe Zod schemy są permissive na obecnych payloadach iOS klienta.
- **Otwarte items (do późniejszych rund):**
  - `next/node_modules/postcss` 2 moderate XSS (transitive) — wymaga upgrade Next albo akceptacji ryzyka (CSS stringify XSS exploitable tylko gdy frontend sam stringify-uje user-controlled CSS, czego Solvio nie robi).
  - drizzle-kit dev dependency — esbuild SSRF (low impact w build-time).
  - Brak CSRF tokenu na state-mutating endpoints (mitygacja: SameSite=Lax na session cookie + custom Origin header w iOS kliencie).
  - Audit log — żadnego DB-poziomu trail dla mutacji (groups, splits, payment requests).
- **Pliki dotknięte:** `next.config.ts`, `lib/cron-auth.ts` (NEW), `app/api/cron/refresh-intel/route.ts`, `app/api/cron/refresh-promotions/route.ts`, `app/api/data/switch-product/route.ts`, `app/api/data/onboarding/route.ts`, `app/api/bank/connect/route.ts`, `app/api/personal/budget/route.ts`, `app/api/settlement/[id]/route.ts`, `app/api/groups/splits/[splitId]/settle/route.ts`, `package.json` + `package-lock.json` (audit fix).

### 2026-05-07 — Round 1 / A3 iOS UI/UX (Expenses list/detail + Groups list)
- **Co:** Polish haptyki + VoiceOver/a11y dla 3 high-traffic ekranów które nie były dotknięte w poprzednich rundach: ExpensesListView (+ ExpenseCreateSheet), ExpenseDetailView (+ ExpenseEditSheet), GroupsListView (+ GroupCreateSheet).
- **Dlaczego:** Audyt UX — Expenses i Groups to dwa najczęściej używane CRUD screens po Dashboard; bulk delete bar i icon-only buttony nie miały a11y labels, sheety nie miały detentów, save callbacks nie miały success haptika, pull-to-refresh nie matchował Dashboard pattern (impact light + success).
- **ExpensesListView:**
  - Pull-to-refresh → Dashboard pattern (`Haptics.impact(.light)` na threshold + `Haptics.success()` po fresh data, gated by `errorMessage == nil`).
  - Create sheet save callback → `Haptics.success()` on success + `Haptics.error()` on failure.
  - Bulk-delete trash button → dodano `accessibilityLabel` + `accessibilityHint`.
  - Search/vendor/amount-clear icon-only buttons → `accessibilityLabel(common.clear)`.
  - Date chips + category chips → `accessibilityAddTraits(.isSelected)` na aktywnej.
  - Header (NBScreenHeader) → `accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` + `.isHeader`.
  - Expense row → `accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` + skomponowany label "title, category, date, amount" + `.isSelected` w bulk mode.
- **ExpenseCreateSheet:**
  - Naprawiono błąd: `presentationDetents` był na wewnętrznym `categoryPicker` zamiast na sheet (efekt: detenty nigdy nie były aktywne). Przeniesione na sheet body + dodano `presentationDragIndicator(.visible)`.
  - Category picker buttony → `accessibilityAddTraits(.isSelected)`.
- **ExpenseDetailView:**
  - Pull-to-refresh → Dashboard pattern (impact light + success on no notFound).
  - Edit sheet save callback → `Haptics.success()` + `Haptics.error()`.
  - Toolbar pencil button → `accessibilityLabel(common.edit)`.
  - Hero card → `accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` + skomponowany label "amount label, amount, title, category, date" + `.isHeader`.
- **ExpenseEditSheet:**
  - Dodano `presentationDetents([.medium, .large])` + `presentationDragIndicator(.visible)`.
  - Category picker buttony → `accessibilityAddTraits(.isSelected)`.
- **GroupsListView:**
  - Pull-to-refresh → Dashboard pattern (impact light + success on no error).
  - Create sheet save callback → `Haptics.success()` + `Haptics.error()`.
  - Delete confirm → `Haptics.warning()` przed action + `Haptics.success()` po realnym delete + `Haptics.error()` na fail.
  - Banner dismiss button → `Haptics.selection()`.
  - Header → `accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` + `.isHeader`.
  - Group card → `accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` + skomponowany label "name, member count, balance" + `.isButton`. Emoji + member avatars → `accessibilityHidden(true)` (powiela info już w label).
  - Open button → `accessibilityLabel(groups.open)` + `accessibilityHint(group.name)`.
  - Recent activity disclosure → `Haptics.selection()` na toggle.
- **GroupCreateSheet:**
  - Emoji picker → `accessibilityLabel(emoji)` + `accessibilityAddTraits(.isSelected)`.
  - Add member button → `Haptics.selection()`.
  - Member-remove icon-only x → `Haptics.warning()` + `accessibilityLabel(common.delete)` + `accessibilityHint(memberName)`.
  - Color picker dots → `accessibilityLabel(hexColor)` + `accessibilityAddTraits(.isSelected)`.
- **Nowe L10n keys:** `common.clear` (PL: "Wyczyść" / EN: "Clear"). Wszystkie inne komunikaty reused z istniejących kluczy (`common.delete`, `common.edit`, `groups.open`, `groups.memberName`, `expenses.confirmBulkDeleteDesc`, etc.).
- **Build:** `xcodebuild -project Solvio.xcodeproj -scheme Solvio -destination generic/platform=iOS -configuration Debug build` → **BUILD SUCCEEDED**.
- **Reguły:** tylko 4 pliki produktowe + L10n.swift (keys), zero edycji `app/`/`components/`/`lib/`. Wszystkie haptyki przez istniejący `Haptics.X` API. Recently-polished views (Analysis/Audit/Prices/Reports/Dashboard/Settings) NIE dotknięte.
- **Pliki:** `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift`, `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift`, `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Groups/GroupsListView.swift`, `native-ios/Solvio/Core/L10n.swift`.

### 2026-05-07 — iOS AI/data screens haptic + UX polish (Analysis/Audit/Prices/Reports)
- **Co:** Pełne pokrycie haptyką + spójność UX dla 4 AI/data screens. Każda interakcja ma teraz adekwatny feedback wibracji.
- **Dlaczego:** Audyt UX — to są ekrany z najdłuższymi loading times w aplikacji (Analysis ~12s, Audit ~18s, Prices ~14s) + Reports z faktycznym file generation. Brak haptyki sprawiał że primary CTA i swipe-to-expand były "deaf".
- **Zmiany per plik:**
  - **AnalysisView:** period segmented `onChange(of: period)` → `Haptics.selection()` (przed cache lookup / run launch). Generate/Regenerate primary CTA → `Haptics.impact(.medium)`. Error retry tap → `Haptics.impact(.light)` (subtle bo error już dostał uwagę toastem). Pull-to-refresh → `Haptics.impact(.light)` na threshold + `Haptics.success()` po fresh data lands (Dashboard pattern).
  - **AuditView:** Generate/Regenerate primary CTA → `Haptics.impact(.medium)` (kicks off ~18s of work, user should feel commit). Error retry → `Haptics.impact(.light)`. Pull-to-refresh light + success. Currency segmented zweryfikowany — był wcześniej usunięty (duplikował Settings), nic do dodania.
  - **PricesView:** Compare/Recompare primary CTA → `Haptics.impact(.medium)`. Error retry → `Haptics.impact(.light)`. Pull-to-refresh light + success. Expand-all toggle → `Haptics.selection()` (discrete pick). Per-card chevron tap → `Haptics.selection()`. Inner pricesOpen "view all prices" toggle → `Haptics.selection()` (same family as card chevron).
  - **ReportsView:** Error retry → `Haptics.impact(.light)`. Pull-to-refresh light + success. Year-card "Show months/Hide months" toggle → `Haptics.selection()` (discrete reveal). `generateIconButton` helper (one shared button function dla yearly + monthly generate) → `Haptics.impact(.medium)` (medium impact bo każdy generate kicks off real server work — PDF/CSV/DOCX build + Vercel Blob upload). Download `Link` taps (PDF/CSV/DOCX year-level + monthly mini-download P/C/D pills) → `Haptics.impact(.medium)` przez `simultaneousGesture(TapGesture)` (nie blokuje native Link URL open).
- **Common:** pull-to-refresh wszędzie ujednolicony do Dashboard pattern (impact light + success on no-error). Empty/error states zweryfikowane — Reports używa `NBEmptyState` (chart.bar.doc.horizontal), Prices używa `NBEmptyState` (doc.text.magnifyingglass), Analysis/Audit nie potrzebują empty bo mają explicit Generate CTA card. Wszystkie primary CTA używają `NBPrimaryButtonStyle`.
- **Build:** 4 edytowane pliki kompilują się czysto bez warningów (jedyny error w buildzie to pre-existing iOS 17 API w `GroupDetailView.swift:812` — niezwiązany z tym taskiem).
- **Reguły:** tylko 4 pliki AI/data dotknięte (zero edycji L10n/Theme/NBComponents/Haptics/Repositories), wszystkie haptyki przez `Haptics.X` API, brak nowych L10n keys (haptyka = runtime feedback, nie tekst).
- **Pliki:** `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Analysis/AnalysisView.swift` (~5 haptic callsites), `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Audit/AuditView.swift` (~3 callsites), `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Prices/PricesView.swift` (~6 callsites), `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Reports/ReportsView.swift` (~6 callsites, w tym 2× `simultaneousGesture` na Link buttons).

### 2026-05-07 — iOS DashboardView UX audit (haptics + a11y labels)
- **Co:** Audyt + polish haptyki i VoiceOver w `Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift` + `DailyStackedChartCard.swift`. Każda interaktywna akcja ma teraz odpowiedni feedback i czytelną etykietę dla VoiceOver.
- **Dlaczego:** Hero KPI ma już `.contentTransition(.numericText())`, ale lista expense rows, segmented (7/14/30D), legend toggle i tap-on-bar nie generowały żadnego haptyka. Dla VoiceOver hero amount nie miał kontekstu (czytał gołe `1234,56 zł`).
- **Zmiany DashboardView:**
  - Refresh button → `Haptics.impact(.light)` + `accessibilityLabel(common.refresh)`.
  - "See all" w recent activity → `Haptics.selection()` + `accessibilityHint`.
  - Expense row tap (NBRow) → `Haptics.selection()` przed `router.push(.expenseDetail)`.
  - AI Insights CTA → `Haptics.selection()`.
  - "Go to expenses" w noRecentDataCard → `Haptics.selection()`; "Scan" → `Haptics.impact(.medium)` (primary CTA).
  - Hero amount → `accessibilityLabel("Total spent: 1234 PLN")` + `.isHeader` trait.
  - Forecast / Savings rate → `accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` + explicit label dla VoiceOver.
- **Zmiany DailyStackedChartCard:**
  - Range segmented (7D/14D/30D) → `Haptics.selection()` only on actual change.
  - Legend toggle (show/hide category) → `Haptics.selection()`.
  - Chart tap (day bar) → `Haptics.impact(.light)` przed `selectedDay`.
  - Expense row w DayBreakdownSheet → `Haptics.selection()`.
- **Verified pass:** Pull-to-refresh już ma `.impact(.light)` + `.success()`, empty state używa `NBEmptyState` z prawidłowym title+subtitle+CTA "Skanuj" → `router.showingScanSheet = true`, hero `.contentTransition(.numericText())` zachowany.
- **Build:** `xcodebuild -scheme Solvio -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17 Pro' -configuration Debug build` → BUILD SUCCEEDED.
- **Reguły:** tylko 2 pliki Dashboard dotknięte (zero edycji L10n/Theme/NBComponents/Haptics). Wszystkie haptyki przez istniejący `Haptics.X` API. **Brak nowych L10n keys** — użyto istniejącego `common.refresh`.

### 2026-05-07 — iOS SettingsView UX polish (haptics + sheet detents)
- **Co:** Dopracowanie haptyki i sheet detents w `Features/Settings/SettingsView.swift`. Wszystkie taktyczne akcje mają teraz odpowiedni feedback wibracji.
- **Dlaczego:** Audyt UX — Settings to drugi najczęściej odwiedzany ekran po Dashboard, a brakowało systemowego haptyka na większości pickerów / przycisków usuwania.
- **Zmiany per sekcja:**
  - **Account card:** tap kopiuje email do schowka + `Haptics.selection()` + toast `settings.emailCopied`. Dodano ikonę `doc.on.doc` jako wskazówkę.
  - **Appearance:** theme picker (system/light/dark/evening) → `Haptics.selection()` na zmianę (filtrowane przez porównanie z poprzednią wartością).
  - **Preferences:** language PL/EN → `Haptics.selection()`, currency chips → `Haptics.selection()` + checkmark dla aktywnej waluty + `accessibilityAddTraits(.isSelected)`.
  - **Categories card:** Add → `Haptics.selection()`, pencil → `Haptics.selection()`, trash → `Haptics.warning()` (+ accessibility labels, 36×36 hit area).
  - **Budgets card:** Add → `Haptics.selection()`, row tap (edit) → `Haptics.selection()`.
  - **Merchant rules:** trash → `Haptics.warning()` przed potwierdzeniem, success haptic po realnym usunięciu.
  - **Export Data:** tap → `Haptics.impact(.medium)`, sukces → `Haptics.success()` po `presentShareSheet`.
  - **CategoryEditorSheet & BudgetEditorSheet:** dodano `presentationDetents([.medium, .large])` + `presentationDragIndicator(.visible)`. Sukces save → `Haptics.success()` w 4 callbackach (create category / edit category / create budget / edit budget). Sukces delete category → `Haptics.success()`.
  - **Sign out:** już miało `Haptics.warning()` (zweryfikowane).
- **Nowe L10n keys:** `settings.copyEmail` (PL: "Skopiuj email" / EN: "Copy email"), `settings.emailCopied` (PL: "Email skopiowany" / EN: "Email copied").
- **Build:** `xcodebuild -project Solvio.xcodeproj -scheme Solvio -destination generic/platform=iOS` → **BUILD SUCCEEDED**.
- **Pliki:** `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Settings/SettingsView.swift`, `native-ios/Solvio/Core/L10n.swift`.
